Tap toggles (`toggleCheck`, undoable) unless dragging/read/pen/eraser/select tool; corner grip resizes; no edit pencil; outline shown only with Select tool. |

`EDIT_FIELDS` (2436) is the undo/reset field set: `title, description, notes, tags, color, layout, text, font, size, bold, italic, align, orient, rot, glow, glowColor, shape, w, h, fill, outline, outlineW, outlineColor, src, round, width, nowrap, style, points, dash, checked`. `STYLE_FIELDS` (format painter) 2186.

## 7. Handwriting (ink) — the part 2.0 must reproduce pixel-for-pixel

### 7.1 Styles and width (224-260)
`PEN_STYLES` = `pen {mul 1, min 1, opacity 1, cap round, taper 0}`, `brush {2.1, 1.5, .95, round, taper .55}`, `pencil {.75, 1, .72, round, taper 0, grain}`, `marker {1.7, 2, .92, square, taper .18, hidden}`, `highlighter {3.4, 6, .3, round, taper 0}`; `blend` is `''` for all (the "multiply" comment is not implemented). Visible order `PEN_ORDER = pencil, pen, brush, highlighter`. `styleWidth(style, pct) = max(min, round10((0.6 + pct/100·22)·mul))` → at the default 12 %: pen 3.2, brush 6.8, pencil 2.4, highlighter 11, marker 5.5; at 100 %: 22.6 / 47.5 / 17 / 76.8 / 38.4; at 1 %: 1 / 1.7 / 1 / 6 / 2. **Stroke width is stored in world units = `curWidth()/scale` (4375)** so on-screen thickness is zoom-independent. Pencil grain = `stroke-dasharray "1.1w 0.55w"` (SVG) / `setLineDash([1.1w, .55w])` (canvas). Taper styles are filled ribbons (`inkTaperD` 315-344: resample at `minGap = max(1.1, .45w)`, 3-tap smooth centreline, window normals, quadratic side curves), stroked styles use midpoint-quadratic `inkPathD` (4257). `nibFactor(vel, pressure, taper) = max(.15, (1 − taper·min(1, vel/2.6)) · (pressure ? .45 + 1.1·pressure : 1))`; `taperWidths` uses the stored per-point factor `p[3]` (geometry fallback for old strokes), tip softening `(0.55 + 0.45·min(1, min(i, n−1−i)/2))`, two 1-2-1 smoothing passes.

### 7.2 Input acceptance (351-391, 4269-4396)
`isPalm`: touch with width or height > 42 or area > 1500 → ignored entirely. `inkAccepts`: pen always (sets `sawStylus`); touch only when `fingerDraw==='on'` (default off; one toast hint per session); mouse always. Pen mode: pointer on `.banner-stack/#pen-bar/#minimap` excluded; while `inking|erasing|lasso`, any pointer failing `inkAccepts` is ignored (no pan, no long-press); pen-down abandons any finger pan/drag/gizmo in flight (`abandonPointer` reverts moves); a second *accepted* pointer mid-stroke drops the stroke and starts a pinch. Pressure is used only when `pointerType==='pen'` (`e.pressure||0.5`). `setPointerCapture` on the stage for ink/eraser only.

### 7.3 Sampling (4226-4253, 7972-7974)
`pointerrawupdate` (window) and `pointermove` both feed `addInkSamples`; coalesced events used when present. De-dup rule: `if (!fromRaw && rawSeen && t <= lastT) skip`. Min step `0.7/scale` world (0.7 screen px). Coordinates rounded to 0.1 world. Velocity = screen px/ms, EMA `0.7·old + 0.3·new`, `dt ≥ 1`. Point = `[x, y, pressure, taper ? round3(nibFactor) : 0]`. **[I]** If a WebView delivers `pointerrawupdate` only for the newest sample while `pointermove.getCoalescedEvents()` carries the intermediate history, the history is dropped (its timestamps are ≤ lastT) — the likely mechanism of symptom 2.

### 7.4 Live surface (4123-4211, styles.css:328-335)
`#ink-live` full-stage 2D canvas, `getContext('2d', {desynchronized:true, alpha:true})`, `dpr = min(devicePixelRatio, 2.5)`, `display:none` except `#stage.penning`, `z-index 6`, `pointer-events:none`, `contain:paint`. `paintLiveStroke` clears and repaints the **whole** stroke as one `Path2D(inkStrokeD(pts))` under `setTransform(dpr·scale, …, dpr·tx, dpr·ty)`, same alpha/cap/join/dash as the SVG. `scheduleLivePaint`: first sample of a frame paints synchronously, the rest coalesce into one rAF paint (≤2 paints/frame; r10test asserts). `applyView`/`resize` repaint it. `dropLiveStroke/dropLiveGestures` on navigation, blur, visibility, second finger.

### 7.5 Commit (`onPointerUp` 4782-4803, `finalizeInk` 5715-5762)
Stroke belonging to another ws/level is discarded. ≥2 points → shape snap if on (§7.7) else `finalizeInk` (not awaited). Block: `bx = round(minX − width − 2)`, `by` likewise, origin `ox = bx + width + 2`; `pts` relative to the origin rounded to **0.05** (`round(v·20)/20`), pressure pushed only if `pr||k` (2 dp), nib factor pushed only if `k` (so entries are length 2, 3 or 4); `w = round(maxX − ox)`, `h = round(maxY − oy)`; `z = INK_Z = 500`; `color/width/style`. Element appended before the DB write; DB write + `recordChange` queued on `afterInkWrites` and skipped if `history.gen` changed. **Word grouping**: join `lastInk.group` when `now − lastInk.at < 900 ms`, `gapX ∈ (−1.2·lineH, 0.45·lineH)`, `|dyMid| < 0.9·lineH`, `lineH = clamp(h, 18, 90)`; else new `uid()` group. `lastInk` reset on level/workspace change.

### 7.6 Eraser (5297-5493)
Modes `normal` (sweep), `stroke`, `lasso`; badge/right-click/long-press menu; `E` toggles; persisted. Works with or without the pen panel; toolbar icon follows mode; slider shows eraser size for normal mode. `eraserRadiusPx = 3 + size·0.35` (screen px; default 24 → 11.4 px), cursor ring shown for mouse/pen. Sweep: for every unlocked ink block on the level whose padded box intersects the sweep, densify segments (step `max(2,R)`, ≤40 per segment), drop points within `R + width/2` of the sweep segment, keep runs of >1 point as new strokes (`inkFromWorldPts`, same style/group), one undo entry per gesture incl. removed connectors and deep children. Stroke eraser: `document.elementFromPoint` → `.block-ink`. Lasso eraser: red loop, stroke removed when ≥50 % of ~24 sampled points fall inside. Finger never erases (inkAccepts).

### 7.7 Shape snapping (3779-4121; `window.__ngShape` test hook)
Toggle `#pen-snap` (persisted). `recognizeShape`: <6 points or `size·scale < 24` or `pathLen·scale < 34` → none; closed when `gap < max(size·.5, pathLen·.16)`; open: line if `pathLen/gap < 1.16` and max deviation `< gap·.09` (any angle, keeps endpoints → rotation); closed: reject if `pathLen > 2.9·(w+h)`; `tidyClosed`, resample 96, `$1`-style template match (`SR_N 64`, `SR_SIZE 250`, templates circle/triangle/square/pentagon/hexagon/star(0.42), all start points step 4, both directions, golden-section rotation ±45°), accept ≥ `SR_ACCEPT 0.80`; `wiggle` > 1.3 rejects (star 2.5); corner tie-break (`findCorners` MIN_TURN 0.62 / sharp 0.85); ellipse error < .085; four corners → diamond (mid-edge tips) or rotated rectangle/square (ratio > .82, angle snapped to 0 within 5°); hug ratio < .42 → diamond. `createRecognizedShape`: pad 2, `outline:true, fill:false, outlineW = max(2, round(strokeW))`, square → equal sides `shape:'rectangle'`, line → `w = length, h = 2·strokeW + 6, rot = atan2`. Toast "Snapped to …".

### 7.8 Draw panel `#pen-bar` (5495-5711, 7869-7921)
Contents: eraser (badge), pen style (badge), 10 `PEN_COLORS` + white + black dots, thickness slider 1–100 % with −/+ steppers (hold repeats every 70 ms after 380 ms) and % label, finger toggle, shape snap, close. Picking a colour or style turns the eraser off. Draggable from any non-input part (8 px threshold), snaps to 7 anchors `tm bm lm rm tl tr bl` (never bottom-right); sides → vertical layout, top/bottom → flat, corners keep orientation; stays between top bar and breadcrumbs; dodges the mini-map; persisted. Options menu `#pen-menu` (style / eraser / lasso) positioned beside or above/below.

## 8. Tools, selection and arrangement
- **Exclusivity** (5233-5295): pen, eraser, Select and Link are one-at-a-time; `dropActiveTools` on add/navigate/home/back/forward; Esc order in §11. `penButton`: eraser up → back to pen, else toggle panel. Read mode blocks all.
- **Select tool** (lasso): stylus/mouse only, finger pans; loop starts on empty canvas (a block under the pen still taps/drags); hit = block centre (`x + offsetWidth/2`) inside polygon; modes `replace | add | remove` (`LASSO_MODES` 3669, icon shows mode, mode resets to replace when the tool is put away); grows to whole groups; toast "N items selected"/"Selection cleared". `#stage.lassoing` hides per-block handles.
- **Other selection**: click selects (`selectBlock` grows groups), shift-click toggles, right-drag marquee (`blockRectOf` intersection, shift adds), Ctrl+A (`selectAllOnLevel`), tap on empty clears, `#btn-delete` dims when nothing selected. `applySelectionClasses` toggles `.selected` on every element and `#world.many-sel` when >12 selected (drops the drop-shadow glow, styles.css:466-469).
- **Groups** (2223-2273): `b.group` id; Ctrl+G / Ctrl+Shift+G, sel-bar buttons; auto groups from handwriting; `withGroups` expands selections.
- **Selection frame** (`#sel-frame`, 1822-1992): shown when Select tool is on or the selection contains ink; box measured from every element's `getBoundingClientRect` (carried as `selFrameBox` during drag/pan/pinch/wheel bursts); 6 px pad; corner grip (22 px on coarse pointers) scales about the box top-left with `ratio = clamp(avg((w+dx)/w, (h+dy)/h), .02, 200)`: ink `pts·ratio`, `w/h·ratio`, `width·ratio (≥.4)`; text size 4–4000 and wrap width; check 12–600; table font 5–400, w≥60, h≥40, colW≥20, rowH≥16; others w/h 8–200000; rAF-throttled; one undo entry; toast "Scaled up/down". Hidden in read mode.
- **Selection bar** (`#sel-bar`, 1782-1820; index.html:100-113): shown for the same condition, above the selection (below if no room): Group, Ungroup, Properties (ink → multi-stroke ink panel; else the block's editor), Copy, Cut, Delete, Send to back, Send backward, Bring forward, Bring to front. (Align buttons were removed; alignment lives in Ctrl+K.)
- **Drag** (4491-4499, 4666-4694, 4836-4867): 3 px threshold; moves whole selection if primary is selected; `snapVal` to `GRID 26` when snap on (ink always plain round); alignment guides (`GUIDE_SNAP 6` screen px, edges/centres vs all other blocks, dashed lines in `#guide-layer`; skipped when only ink moves); edges redrawn per move only if attached; one undo entry; `justDragged` suppresses the click. Unselected ink under the pointer → pan instead (`inkPassThrough`), tap picks the word group. Locked blocks select but never move/resize.
- **Z-order** (1641-1697): `reorderZ` to front/back (max+1 / min−n), `stepZ` one step (normalises z to 0..n−1 first — INK_Z is lost after any step), Ctrl+]/[ and Ctrl+Shift+]/[, ctx menu, sel-bar.
- **Lock** (1700), **Nudge** arrows 1 px / Shift 10 px (450 ms debounced undo entry), **Tab** adds a sibling 40 px right, **Align/distribute** left/center/right/top/middle/bottom/dist-h/dist-v (cmdk), **Tidy** (`tidyLevel` 6728: selection or level, skips ink/locked, grid `GAP 40`, `perRow = ceil(sqrt n)`), **Format painter** Ctrl+Alt+C/V (`STYLE_FIELDS`).
- **Clipboard** (2314-2429): deep snapshot (descendants, internal edges, sibling edges, files); paste offsets +28 px, fresh ids, `ws` reassigned, files reuse Blobs; Ctrl+C/X/V/D; ctx "Paste" disabled without clipboard.

## 9. Connectors (941-1037, 5765-5801)
Link mode (`L`, `#btn-link`, banner "Click two blocks to connect them" + Done): first tap marks `.link-src`, second creates `{id, ws, parentId, from, to, createdAt}` unless already connected either way; same block cancels. Drawn in `#edge-layer` SVG as `g.edge-g` with a 16 px transparent hit path, `path.edge` (1.8 px, `marker-end #arrow`, `marker-start` when `both`), optional `<text.edge-label>` at the midpoint. Endpoints = border intersection from centre to centre (`borderPoint`). Styles `curve` (quadratic via midpoint), `straight`, `elbow` (mid-x). Click → `#edge-modal` (label, Curved/Straight/Elbow, arrows both ends, Remove w/ confirm, Cancel, Save). Edges redraw on every `drawEdges` (full rebuild). Level export/PDF draw them.

## 10. Context menus and long-press (4933-5002, 4509-4536)
Right-click (mouse) or press-and-hold 500 ms (touch, or stylus outside pen/eraser; cancelled by >8 px move, pinch, or a live stylus gesture). On a block: Edit, Open inside (cards only), Copy N, Cut, | Bring to front, Bring forward, Send backward, Send to back, | Lock/Unlock, Duplicate, | Delete N (danger). Blank canvas: Paste, Fit to view, | Mini-map on/off, Lock to horizontal, Lock to vertical, Snap to grid, Autosave, | Workspace properties, About. On a table cell/title the hold/right-click opens the table editor on that cell instead. Menu clamps to the viewport; closes on outside pointerdown/wheel/Esc.

## 11. Keyboard, command palette, menus
**Keys** (`bindKeys` 7659-7762; table cells own the keyboard while `editTableId`): Ctrl/Cmd+K palette (always); Esc cascade: presenting → cmdk → pen menu → search → ctxmenu → prompt → props → confirm → about → pen mode → eraser → select tool → link mode → text/shape/image/ink drawers → block drawer → clear selection → hide search results. Not while typing / no workspace: Ctrl +/=/−/0 zoom; Ctrl+S save; Ctrl+Z undo; Ctrl+Y / Ctrl+Shift+Z redo; Ctrl+A select all; Ctrl+C/X/V; Ctrl+Alt+C/V copy/paste look; Ctrl+D duplicate; Ctrl+]/[ (+Shift) z-order; arrows nudge; N new block; L link; P pen; E eraser; Tab sibling; Ctrl+G / Ctrl+Shift+G group/ungroup; presenting: →/Space/PageDown next, ←/PageUp prev; F11 fullscreen; O outline; M minimap; F fit; Delete/Backspace delete selected; `/` search.
**Two-finger-tap undo / three-finger-tap redo were removed** (r4test asserts `twoFingerTapNoUndo`, `threeFingerTapNoRedo`).
**Ctrl+K palette** (`cmdCommands` 5006-5058, block search over title/text/notes/tags, 8 hits): Create: Add block/list/text/shape/image, Import text file · View: Fit, Workspace home, Read mode, Full screen, Mini-map, Lock horizontal/vertical, Outline, Present · Edit: Undo, Redo · Workspace: Export workspace, Export level PNG/SVG, Export PDF, Properties, Snap to grid, New workspace, Import workspace, All workspaces · Style: Copy/Paste look · Arrange: Group, Ungroup, 6 aligns, 2 distributes, Tidy · Ink: Convert handwriting to text · App: Toggle theme, Help, About.
**⋯ menu** (index.html:571-592): Fit, Full screen (state), Mini-map (state), Autosave (state), Lock horizontal/vertical (state), Reset zoom 100 %, Snap to grid (state), Workspace properties | Export workspace, Export as PDF, Export level as PNG/SVG, Outline sidebar, Tidy, Present | About, Help.
**Add menu** (hover on mouse, tap on touch): Block, List, Text, Shape, Checkbox, Import ▸ Images / Import file (.txt .md → text, .csv → list) / Import Excel (.xlsx .csv → table). Drop onto canvas routes the same way (+28 px per file). In-canvas menus never import a workspace `.json` (only Home → Import and cmdk "Import workspace").

## 12. Panels and overlays
- **Outline** (`#outline`, 6676-6724): left 264 px frosted tree of containers only (text/shape/image/ink/table/check skipped), sorted y then x, count badge, current level highlighted, click navigates, refreshed 250 ms after changes, dismissed by outside tap (breadcrumbs excepted); O key.
- **Search** (`/`, `#search-pop` 6024-6127): popover under its button, 160 ms debounce, searches **all workspaces** (title 3, description/text/tags 2, file name 1.5, notes 1, +0.5 current ws; skips shape/image/ink/check), 40 results, other-workspace tag, `goToBlock` switches workspace/level, centres and opens the editor.
- **Read mode** (2173-2182): no editing/drawing/dragging, dblclick still opens cards, `#app.reading` hides actions/handles and dims tool buttons.
- **Presenting** (6762-6791): stops = every level with children (depth-first, y/x order), `#present-bar` prev/next/exit with "title · i / n", hides top/bottom bars, mini-map, banners; navigates with fit.
- **Tag filter** banner, **Link** banner (`.banner-stack` bottom centre).
- **Toast** (7489): bottom centre frosted, 2.2 s, one at a time. **Confirm** (OK focused, Enter confirms, danger/primary) and **Prompt** (name + optional colours/templates/PDF theme) modals; **About/Help** two-tab modal (About: name, colour, blocks, created, file location with reveal/relink, storage estimate, `build N · app|web` from the `?v=` tag).
- Drawers slide from the right (440 px; 400 px ≤1024; 100 % ≤560); exactly one editor open at a time (`closeOtherEditors`).

## 13. Undo/redo semantics (1480-1618)
Entries `{level, before:{blocks,edges,files}, after:{…}}`, shallow record clones, limit 200, per workspace session (`clearHistory` on open/home; `history.gen` guards late ink writes). `undo/redo` run on the `afterInkWrites` chain, flush pending editor timers, close editors, `applyDelta` (put target, delete records only in the other set), then `applyRecsToView` (in-place: remove/replace elements for this level only, re-read fresh records from IDB, recount parents, reload edges; if the level is gone → root). Toasts Undone/Redone/Nothing to undo/redo; buttons + Ctrl+Z/Y + cmdk. Undoable: create, delete (deep capture), move, scale grip, gizmo, nudge, z-order, lock, group/ungroup, align/tidy/paste-look, edges (create/edit/delete), paste/duplicate/cut, editor sessions (one entry), ink strokes, eraser gestures, ink panel sessions, table sessions, check toggles, colour/row/col resizes, image replace, convert-to-text. Not undoable: file attach/remove, workspace properties.

## 14. Exports (6129-6674)
- Workspace JSON (§2.3): shell → native Save As + `writeFile(string)`, web → download. Prompted for a name.
- **PDF** (`exportWorkspacePdf`, pdf.js): prompt name + Light/Dark page style; A4 landscape 842×595 pt, margin 34, header breadcrumb trail + "n / total" + title + rule at y 58; one page per level that has children (root always), depth-first in y/x order; content scaled to fit (≤1.6×), connectors first (straight, arrow head 6.5), per-kind drawing (text, check, image JPEG ≤1400 px on page background, ink smooth/taper polygon with opacity, shapes incl. dashed lines, tables, cards with accent strip, description, plain-text notes, tags, meta); blocks with pages get GoTo link annotations and a "p.N" badge; bookmark outline (`/PageMode/UseOutlines`); Helvetica/Helvetica-Bold WinAnsi, ExtGState alpha. Saved via NGShell binary write or download.
- **Level PNG/SVG** (`levelToSvg`): current level only, PAD 40, theme colours, connectors with labels, per-kind SVG; PNG rasterised at 2×; names `<ws>-<level|home>.svg|png`.

## 15. Responsive layout (styles.css:1140-1315)
Desktop: 3-column grid top bar (`--bar-h 64`): left = brand, outline/search/read, divider, home/back/forward; centre = Add, Link, Pen, Eraser(badge), Select(badge), Delete; right = undo, redo, zoom −/label/+, divider, save pill, theme, More. ≤1024: no wordmark, smaller buttons, zoom label hidden, drawer 400. ≤960: bar wraps. ≤860: dividers hidden, minimap 150×104. ≤560: `--bar-h 56`, three rows (left / tools / right), zoom buttons hidden, breadcrumbs scroll, drawers 100 %, menus ≤100vw−20, single-column home, minimap 130×90, tighter pen bar. Phones only (`hover:none and pointer:coarse and (max-width or max-height ≤560)`): drawers become **bottom sheets** (`--sheet-h` default 40vh, drag grip 18–85vh, shared across panels, preview/dropzone/tips hidden, inline toggle rows). `hover:none`: 34 px action buttons, 26 px handles, 42 px toolbar icons, 30 px swatches, fat always-visible col/row grips. ≤480 tall: minimap hidden. Tests run at 1440, 1024, 800 and 390 px.

## 16. Regression suite and perf baseline to keep green
Headless Edge over CDP against `http://localhost:8765/` (scratchpad `*test.ps1`, run with `bash suite.sh …`). Expected non-failures: `fatal=false` everywhere, `fatal2=false` (audit), `inkMovedOnPan=false` (r3), `shapeSnapStillWorks=false` (verifytest, stale), `hasDest=false` (pdftest). Key pinned behaviours: r10 (raw+coalesced de-dup 55–62 points for 60 samples, ≤2 live paints/frame, resting finger ignored, undo right after a stroke, in-place undo, erase-then-undo, ink svg node reused, editor flush on close, many-sel glow gate, `--inv` deferral, image bound webp <60 %), r11 (pen down abandons finger drag/gizmo, delete with pending edit, Home mid-stroke discards the stroke, history cleared on reopen), r4 (eraser split/rejoin, three erasers, panel snapping, finger defaults, no tap-undo), r2/r2b (frame + grip scaling incl. pressure kept), r3 (ink pans unless picked, multi-stroke restyle), b1 (guides, groups, align, paper), b23 (edges, outline, present, tidy, PNG/SVG), pdftest, sheettest (bottom sheets), verifytest (all styles live + saved, surface clear after). Perf (`perfbench-final.txt`, v113, dpr 1): per-move JS at point 1450 ≈ 0.0 ms med / 0.9 ms p95 (1.1 at dpr 2.5), finalize ≈ 4.6 ms, undo 1500-pt stroke 5 ms; 606 blocks: minimap ≈ 2 ms/frame, zoom click forced layout ≈ 13 ms med; 2206 blocks: minimap ≈ 7 ms, zoom forced layout ≈ 36 ms med, undo/redo ≈ 9–23 ms med; 300 strokes add 900 nodes; lasso select of ~100 strokes ≈ 10 ms; payload of 2207 blocks (39 430 ink points, 1.4 MB images) = 2.4 MB JSON, 13 ms to build.

## 17. Existing quirks / dead code (decide, don't silently change)
1. Export drops edge `label/style/both` and workspace `paper`; paste also drops edge styling (2413).
2. Changing an ink block's `width` (panel or scale grip) shifts the drawn stroke by the width delta because points are relative to `x + width + 2` **[I]**; the grip also drifts by `2·(1−ratio)` px.
3. z=0 blocks stack in UUID order; `stepZ` renumbers all z (INK_Z floor lost).
4. Highlighter has no blend mode despite comments; marker style hidden but renderable.
5. Dead: `penWidth`/`ng-pen-width`, `meta` store, `data-act="add-child"` handler, `[data-sel="paint"]` reference, `data-align` sel-bar handler.
6. In-canvas Import menu cannot import a workspace `.json` (Home/cmdk only) — relevant to the "exports not importing" report.
7. Two/three-finger tap undo/redo intentionally removed; `window.__ngShape` is the only debug hook (2.0 plans `window.__ng`).
8. Selection frame/bar and lasso hit-testing read layout (`getBoundingClientRect/offsetWidth`) for every selected/level block; `drawEdges` rebuilds all connector nodes each call; `applySelectionClasses` touches every element — the measured sources of symptom 3.

Findings:

PDF, SVG export, minimap and eraser all recompute world points as x + pad + p[0]. Recommendations:
- Keep the ink record format byte-compatible: `pts` entries of length 2-4 at 0.05 precision relative to (x + width + 2, y + width + 2), `w/h = round(max - origin)`, `z = 500`, `group` from the 900 ms / 0.45-lineH rule, and `width` in world units = slider px / view.scale. Write a shared codec module used by the renderer, eraser, scale grip, minimap, PDF and SVG export.
- Lift PEN_STYLES, styleWidth, nibFactor/taperWidths/taperCentreline/inkTaperD/inkPathD and the shape recogniser into pure functions with golden tests generated from v113 output (path `d` strings per style), so the live layer, tiles and exports are verified pixel/geometry-identical before any UI work.
- Replace timestamp-based de-dup with identity/sequence-based intake: consume coalesced events from pointermove (or rawupdate) but never both for the same sample; count dropped samples and expose them in the diagnostics overlay so the Samsung symptom can be confirmed on device.
- Render committed ink to a tiled canvas layer and keep only typed content (cards, text, tables, images, shapes, connectors) in the DOM; keep the CSS selection look (accent glow up to 12 items, dashed outline beyond) by drawing it in the ink layer, and compute selection frames from stored geometry rather than getBoundingClientRect.
- Preserve every interaction rule inventoried here as an explicit spec table in the 2.0 repo (palm 42 px / 1500 px², finger-draw default off, long-press 500 ms / 8 px, drag 3 px, tap 4 px, GUIDE_SNAP 6, GRID 26, zoom 0.02-64, eraser radius 3 + 0.35·size, lasso centre-in-polygon, tool exclusivity, Esc cascade) and port the r1-r11/b/verify tests through a `window.__ng` API before deleting `.block-ink` elements.
- Decide the data-loss quirks up front: preserve edge label/style/both and workspace paper in export and paste (backward compatible), keep INK_Z semantics stable across stepZ, and either keep or migrate the width-shift behaviour with an explicit origin field.
- Keep storage semantics: IndexedDB writes immediately, per-record; autosave stays a debounced whole-file write of the same JSON with the same pill states, but batch IDB writes in one transaction (bench: 0.12 ms/rec vs 0.30 ms/rec) and consider an incremental save only if the file format stays identical.
- Do not reintroduce two/three-finger tap undo; remove the dead code (penWidth, meta store, add-child, paint/align sel-bar handlers) but keep the hidden marker style renderable for old strokes.
- Carry the shell unchanged into 2.0.0: NGShell surface, Windows Ink recogniser, Android immersive MainActivity patch, targetSdk 35 opt-out, SW stripped in app builds, `?v` = sw.js CACHE on the website, and a Tauri version bump in both tauri.conf.json and Cargo.toml.
Open questions:
- On the Samsung tablet, does the Android WebView fire pointerrawupdate for every digitiser sample or only the latest per frame? The timestamp de-dup (app.js:4237) drops coalesced history only in the second case; instrumenting dropped-sample counts in the planned diagnostics overlay would settle whether this is symptom 2's cause.
- Should 2.0 fix the export format's silent losses (edge label/style/both, workspace paper) while keeping `version: 2`, or bump to version 3 with a reader that still accepts 2? Adding fields is backward compatible for the current reader, which ignores unknown keys.
- Should the ink-width-shift quirk (points relative to x + width + 2, so restyling width moves the stroke) be preserved for fidelity with old files or corrected with a migration that rebases old records to an explicit origin?
- Which DOM contracts must survive for the regression suite: the tests select `.block-ink` elements, read `#world` transform and `--inv`, and count DOM nodes; the 2.0 plan needs a `window.__ng` API before strokes stop being elements.
- Is the in-canvas Import menu expected to accept `.notesgallery.json` (today only Home → Import and Ctrl+K do), and does `NGShell.readFile` on Android receive a `content://` URI from the dialog that `readTextFile` cannot open? Both are candidates for the outstanding 'exports not importing' report.
- The highlighter comment says it multiplies into the page but `blend` is '' for every style; is the current plain 30 % alpha the intended look to preserve, or should 2.0 implement multiply?
- Does the eraser need to work while the pen panel is closed on a canvas renderer without `document.elementFromPoint` (stroke mode today depends on DOM hit testing)?

# Notes Gallery regression suite - inventory and 2.0 port analysis

**Paths.** Suite: `C:/Users/AKSHAY~1/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/G--Other-computers-My-Computer-LAT-WorkSpace-Notes/e51a2c53-7f9e-4364-9a18-04f4263008e5/scratchpad/` (abbreviated `<SP>/` below). App: `G:/Other computers/My Computer/LAT_WorkSpace/Notes/Interface/` (`js/app.js` 8109 lines, `js/db.js` 204, `js/pdf.js` 348, `js/platform.js` 67, `css/styles.css` 1315, `index.html` 810, `sw.js` 43; sw CACHE `notes-gallery-v113`, Tauri `version 1.5.3`).

**Scale.** 120 `.ps1` files in the scratchpad; 65 match `*test*.ps1`; the active suite is the 24 names in `suite.sh` + `perfbench.ps1` = 25 scripts, ~830 `log()` keys. Last run (`<SP>/suite-final.txt`, 2026-09-08 15:09) is green except three known-false keys: `r3test inkMovedOnPan=false` (informational - expected false), `verifytest shapeSnapStillWorks=false`, `pdftest hasDest=false`.

## 1. Harness shape (identical in every script)

PowerShell launches headless Edge (`--headless=new --disable-gpu`, fresh profile, unique CDP port), `Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride` to one of four viewports (1440x900 dpr1 desktop; 1100x800 dpr1 `mobile:true`; 390x844 dpr2 phone; 1024x768 in r6ttest), `setTouchEmulationEnabled(5)`, `Page.navigate http://localhost:8765/`, sleeps 3 s, then **one** `Runtime.evaluate` of an async IIFE returning `key=value | key=value`. `suite.sh` greps `=false|EXC|PSERR|PARTIAL|ERR=` from the last two lines. The IIFE:

* seeds data by **raw IndexedDB writes** to `blocknotes` stores `workspaces`, `blocks`, `edges`, `handles` using the on-disk record shape directly (`{id, ws, parentId:'__root__', kind:'ink', pts:[[x,y,p,k]], w,h,width,color,style,x,y,z}`);
* navigates by `#brand` click then `.ws-card[data-ws=..]` click;
* drives input with synthetic `PointerEvent`s: `pointerdown` dispatched on `#stage` (or directly on a `.block[data-id]`, `.tnode-resize`, `#sel-frame .sel-grip`, `.check-face`, `.pen-sep`), `pointermove`/`pointerup` on `window`; fields used: `pointerId, pointerType 'pen'|'touch'|'mouse', isPrimary, width/height (palm = 70x60), pressure, button/buttons, shiftKey`; r10 also dispatches `pointerrawupdate` on `window` and builds `pointermove` with `coalescedEvents:[...]`;
* reads back: `.block-ink` count / `getBoundingClientRect` / `svg path` `d|fill|opacity` / svg node identity; `.block.selected` count; `#sel-frame`/`.sel-grip`/`#sel-bar` rects; `#world` `style.transform`, `--inv`, `.many-sel`; `#stage` classes `penning|erasing|erase-normal|lassoing|panning`; `#ink-live` `getImageData` and `getContextAttributes().desynchronized`; `#toast` text; `#fatal-banner` absence; IDB `blocks` `get/getAll`; `window.__ngShape` (shapebench only); localStorage `ng-pen-style|ng-pen-size|ng-eraser-mode|ng-finger-draw|ng-pen-bar-pos|ng-shape-snap|ng-autosave|ng-snap|ng-minimap`.

Timing: the 24 functional scripts contain 197.7 s of literal `wait()` sleeps (r4 25.9 s, r5 16.7 s, r10 14.9 s) plus 24 x (3 s navigate + ~3 s Edge boot) = roughly 6 min; perfbench runs separately (P4 capped at 150 s).

## 2. Active-suite inventory

Columns: **logs** = `log()` keys; **ink-DOM** = occurrences of `.block-ink` / dispatch-on-ink-element / svg-path reads / `.block(-ink).selected` reads; **try** = wrapped in try/catch (returns `PARTIAL[...]`) vs none (first null read aborts the whole script with `EXC`).

Ink-DOM coupling appears in brushtest, drawtest-desktop, erasetest, fixtest, fix2test, grptest, importtest, lassotest, livetest, pentest-phone*, perftest, r3ptest, shapetest, stresstest, thicktest, v76test (16 of 42).

## 3. (a) What breaks if committed ink moves to a canvas

Coupling classes found in the active suite (70 literal `.block-ink` references in 15 of 25 scripts; 10 scripts have zero ink-DOM coupling: r11, r9, r7, shapebench, r1, r1p, b1, b23, sheettest, pdftest):

1. **`.block-ink` element count as stroke count** - r10 (redoBringsItBack, noFragmentsLeft), shape2 (scribbleStaysInk, handwritingStaysInk), r2 (7 sites), r2b (4), drawtest (9), pentest (10), tooltest (penDrawsAfterEraser/Colour), verifytest (allStylesSaved, palmRejectFnAlive, eraserStillWorks), audit (hasInk + 4 `#world .block` counts), perfbench (ink_blocks_*, rendered_blocks, nodes_added_by_300_strokes=900, and `B.openWs` completion). 
2. **`getBoundingClientRect()` of an ink element** for hit coordinates / frame comparisons / lasso loop bounds - r10 F, r5, r4 (ir, ir2, sorted rects), r3 (6 reads; frameFollowsDuringDrag, frameSettledOnInk), r2 (frameWrapsInk, loop bounds), r2b, drawtest, tooltest (inkDragged), r6 (finalMask, w0), matchtest (finalThicknessAt).
3. **Pointer events dispatched on the ink element** (or `elementFromPoint` returning it) - r3 x3, r5 x2, r6 x2, tooltest x4 (+click, dblclick), pentest dblclick, r10 dblclick, drawtest eraser, verifytest eraser, fix3 `drag('k1')`. In 2.0 these must become stage-level events at `__ng.inkScreenRect(id)` centre - which is also how a real pen behaves, so the port improves realism.
4. **Reading the committed SVG** - `svg path d` (drawtest smoothPath; verifytest curves/serration/avgTurn; brushtest; perfbench saved_path_d_chars), `path` `fill/opacity` (pentest, verifytest), svg node identity (r10 inkSvgReused), rasterising a cloned SVG to pixels (r6 `*_exact` x4, matchtest `*_matches` x4).
5. **Selection styling on ink elements** - `getComputedStyle(.block-ink.selected).filter` (r10 manySelNoGlow, tooltest inkGlow), `.block.selected` counts that include ink (r2, r2b, r3, r6, r10, tooltest, audit/pdftest Ctrl+A), `inkEl.style.zIndex` (fix3 inkZ/inkAboveCards), `#world.many-sel` (r10; survives if the class stays on `#world`).
6. **`#ink-live` 2D probes** - `getImageData` (r9, r6, matchtest, verifytest, livetest, perftest, perfbench flush probes), `getContextAttributes().desynchronized` (perftest), `CanvasRenderingContext2D.prototype.stroke/fill` patch keyed on `canvas.id==='ink-live'` (r10 paintCoalesced). These survive only if 2.0 keeps an `HTMLCanvasElement#ink-live` with a readable 2D context; a WebGL or worker/OffscreenCanvas live layer breaks all of them.

**Failure mode is abort, not degrade.** 11 of the 15 coupled scripts have no try/catch (r10, r6, r5, r4, r3, r2, r2b, audit, verifytest, matchtest, perfbench): the first `querySelector('.block-ink')` returning null throws (`.getBoundingClientRect`/`.dataset` on null) and the whole script reports `EXC`, losing every later key. Those 11 scripts hold 467 of the ~830 logged keys (56%). The 5 coupled scripts with try/catch (shape2, drawtest, pentest, tooltest, fix3) degrade to `PARTIAL`.

## 4. (b) Proposed stable test/debug API: `window.__ng`

Always installed (cheap getters), counters armed by `__ng.enable()`; keep `window.__ngShape` as-is. Vanilla-JS object attached at the end of the IIFE next to `__ngShape` (`app.js:4074`).

```js
window.__ng = {
  version: '2.0.x',
  // --- state (read-only snapshots; never the live objects) ---
  state()            -> { ws, level, levelLayout, view:{scale,tx,ty}, penMode, penEraser, eraserMode, selectTool, lassoMode, readOnly, penStyle, penColor, penSize }
  blocks(filter?)    -> [clone]        // state.blocks on the current level; filter {kind, parentId}
  block(id)          -> clone | null
  inks()             -> [clone]        // replaces document.querySelectorAll('.block-ink')
  selection()        -> [id]           // replaces .block.selected; selectionMode() -> 'glow'|'outline'  (the >12 rule)
  els()              -> {id: HTMLElement}  // non-ink blocks only (cards, text, shape, image, table, check stay DOM)
  // --- geometry (world <-> screen) ---
  worldToScreen(x,y), screenToWorld(sx,sy)
  inkBounds(id)      -> {x,y,w,h} world incl. nib pad   // replaces b.w/b.h + pad maths
  inkScreenRect(id)  -> {left,top,right,bottom,width,height} client px   // replaces el.getBoundingClientRect()
  hitTest(clientX, clientY, {tol}) -> id | null                          // replaces elementFromPoint/.closest('.block-ink')
  selectionBox()     -> world box used for #sel-frame (frame stays a DOM element: #sel-frame/.sel-grip keep working)
  // --- stroke rendering parity ---
  strokePathD(id)    -> string          // same generator the renderer uses (inkStrokeD): replaces svg path d reads
  strokeStyle(id)    -> { filled, opacity, cap, dash, color, width, blend }   // replaces path fill/opacity attrs
  renderStroke(id, ctx, {scale,tx,ty,dpr}) // paints one committed stroke into a caller canvas via the production code path
  // --- pixels ---
  liveCanvas()       -> HTMLCanvasElement (#ink-live)  ; live() -> { mode:'desync2d'|'2d'|'webgl'|'offscreen', dpr, w, h, engaged:boolean }
  readLivePixels(x,y,w,h)  -> ImageData  (screen px; works for any backend via readPixels/transferToImageBitmap)
  readInkPixels(x,y,w,h)   -> ImageData  (committed layer(s) composited, screen px)   // replaces SVG rasterising in r6/matchtest
  tiles()            -> [{key,x,y,size,version,dirty,strokes}]  ; tileAt(worldX, worldY)
  // --- instrumentation ---
  enable()/disable(); counters() -> { livePaints, tileRepaints, strokesCommitted, samples:{raw, coalesced, dedupDropped, minStepDropped, kept}, layoutsForced }
  reset(); on('stroke:begin'|'stroke:sample'|'stroke:commit'|'stroke:drop'|'tile:repaint', fn) -> off
  // --- sync ---
  flush()            -> Promise   // resolves after pending ink writes + history record (afterInkWrites) + one rAF: replaces the 197 s of wait()
  saved(ws?)         -> Promise<[block]> // IDB read via DB, or keep the raw indexedDB helpers
  // --- input replay ---
  injectSamples(pointerId, [{x,y,t,p,tiltX,tiltY}], {channel:'raw'|'coalesced'|'move'}) // Android-batch replay without PointerEvent init quirks
  recordSamples(on)  -> [] // on-device trace capture; dump via toast/clipboard/IDB meta
}
```

Port = mechanical substitution (approximate counts across the active suite): `querySelectorAll('.block-ink').length` -> `__ng.inks().length` (~45); `[...querySelectorAll('.block-ink')].pop()` -> `__ng.inks().at(-1)` (~10); `inkEl.getBoundingClientRect()` -> `__ng.inkScreenRect(id)` (~20); `inkEl.dispatchEvent(pointerdown)` -> `stage.dispatchEvent(pointerdown at rect centre)` (~17); `svg path` reads -> `__ng.strokePathD/strokeStyle` (~9); SVG rasterise -> `__ng.readInkPixels` or `__ng.renderStroke` (2 scripts, 8 keys); `.block.selected` -> `__ng.selection()` (~25); computed-filter glow -> `__ng.selectionMode()` (2); `wait(n)` -> `await __ng.flush()` (~400 sites, optional). Keep everything else: IDB seeding (record shape unchanged), `#brand`/`.ws-card`, all toolbar/panel ids, `#stage` classes, `#sel-frame/.sel-grip/#sel-bar`, `#world` transform + `--inv` + `.many-sel`, `#ink-live` id, `#toast`, `#fatal-banner`, `#import-input/#image-input`, `URL.createObjectURL` capture, `window.NGPdf`, `window.__ngShape`. Recommended: a 60-line `ngtest.js` shim evaluated before each body so bodies change by text replacement only.

## 5. (c) New tests 2.0 needs

1. **Sample intake, Android-style batches** (`__ng.injectSamples` + real PointerEvents): (i) raw channel delivers a subset, next `pointermove.coalescedEvents` carries new samples whose `timeStamp <= lastRaw.timeStamp` -> all kept (today's `t <= inking.lastT` rule at `app.js:4237` drops them); (ii) consecutive samples with **equal** timestamps (ms-quantised) -> none dropped; (iii) coalesced-only at 60 Hz with 6-8 samples/frame (no raw, as WebView/Firefox/Safari) -> kept; (iv) 240 Hz fast curve, 12 px spacing -> committed pts >= 95% of unique inputs and max gap between consecutive committed points <= 1.2x input spacing (catches "straight line to where the pen ended"); (v) pressure/tilt preserved per point; (vi) `counters().samples.dedupDropped === 0` for (i)-(iv); (vii) golden traces recorded on the Samsung via `recordSamples()` replayed -> committed pts identical to reference.
2. **Live layer engaged / fallback**: `live().mode` reported; after pointerdown + 3 samples, `readLivePixels` > 0 within **one** rAF (not `wait(120)`); 50 random strokes -> preview present for every stroke (today's r9 highlighter check generalised to all styles, both blend modes, at dpr 1/2/2.5); with `getContext('2d',{desynchronized:true})` mocked to return non-desync attrs the fallback still paints; resize/dpr change mid-stroke redraws; commit hand-off has no gap or double-draw: on the commit frame `readLivePixels ∪ readInkPixels` coverage >= pre-commit live coverage and after +1 frame live is fully clear.
3. **Pixel-equivalence live vs committed** (port r6/matchtest onto `readInkPixels`): per style x zoom {0.5,1,2.5} x dpr {1,2,2.5}: coverage both ways >= 96%, pixel count within 8%, mid-stroke thickness within 18%; after a pan of (dx,dy) the committed layer equals the pre-pan live layer translated.
4. **Large-selection drag timing**: seed 2000 strokes, lasso 300, 60 drag moves with rAF between: per-move handler median <= 2 ms, p95 <= 4 ms; CDP `LayoutCount` delta during the drag == 0 (no forced layout: today `positionSelFrame -> selectionWorldBox` calls `getBoundingClientRect()` per selected element right after writing `left/top`, `app.js:1833-1850, 4683-4691`); frame-gap p95 <= 20 ms; frame + bar follow; after drop all 300 IDB positions updated and exactly one history entry; `tileRepaints` <= tiles touched by source ∪ destination bbox.
5. **Lasso-select cost and artefacts**: lasso enclosing 1000 strokes: `lasso_up_select_ms` <= 16 ms and `LayoutCount` delta <= 1 (today ~40 layouts per lasso from `selectInsideLasso` `offsetWidth` reads + class toggles, perfbench P6); highlight only changes pixels inside selected strokes' bboxes (diff `readInkPixels` before/after outside those bboxes == 0); `selectionMode()` flips to `outline` above the threshold.
6. **Tile invalidation**: commit -> only intersecting tiles bump `version`; erase-split -> pixels of the removed run gone within 1 frame and a 1-px ring around neighbouring tiles unchanged; undo/redo -> same tile set; zoom step -> visible tiles re-rasterised first, `tileRepaints` <= visible tiles; move selection -> source+destination tiles only; tiles outside viewport+margin evicted (`tiles().length` <= cap); no stale tile after `applyView` (pixel compare against `renderStroke` reference at 8 random points).
7. **Per-frame cost independence** (perfbench P2/P8 extended): at 606 / 2206 / 10 000 strokes, pan-move handler ms, zoom forced-flush ms, minimap ms, undo/redo ms must grow < 1.3x; `#world` child count constant; heap growth bounded.
8. **Autosave**: after one stroke, bytes written / records touched proportional to the stroke, not the 2.42 MB whole-workspace JSON (perfbench P7 at 2207 blocks); serialisation main-thread time <= 5 ms or off-thread; file format (`{app:'NotesGallery',kind:'workspace',version:2,...}`) still round-trips (importtest).
9. **Hit-test parity**: `hitTest` at stroke centre returns the id, at 2x width away returns null; tap-select, stroke-erase, lasso, group pick-up produce the same results as r3/r4/r2/r6 today but via stage-level events.


Findings:
- [high] The active suite is 25 scripts (24 in suite.sh + perfbench) with ~830 logged keys, all driven through one CDP Runtime.evaluate per script; the pass criterion is the absence of '=false|EXC|PSERR|PARTIAL|ERR=' in the last two output lines. Last run is green apart from three known-false keys. - suite.sh runs each <name>.ps1 and greps '[a-z0-9_]*=false|EXC|PSERR|PARTIAL|ERR='. suite-final.txt (2026-09-08 15:09) shows only r3test inkMovedOnPan=false (an informational key whose expected value is false), verifytest shapeSnapStillWorks=false and pdftest hasDest=false; every 'fatal=false' is the #fatal-banner flag, i.e. a pass. 120 .ps1 files exist in the scratchpad, 65 match *test*.ps1; audit.ps1, shapebench.ps1 and perfbench.ps1 are active but do not match the glob. (C:/Users/AKSHAY~1/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/G--Other-computers-My-Computer-LAT-WorkSpace-Notes/e51a2c53-7f9e-4364-9a18-04f4263008e5/scratchpad/suite.sh and suite-final.txt) => Established baseline: 2.0 must reproduce the same key set, and the harness (headless Edge + CDP + raw IndexedDB seeding + synthetic PointerEvents) can be kept unchanged; only the in-page readbacks need a stable API.
- [high] Every script seeds state by writing raw records into the 'blocknotes' IndexedDB stores (workspaces/blocks/edges/handles) using the on-disk field names, and reads results back the same way; DB.js schema v3 has stores workspaces, blocks, edges, files, meta, handles with indexes ws/parentId/blockId. - e.g. r10test: put('blocks',{id:'c1',ws:'r10',parentId:'__root__',...}); inks=()=>(await all()).filter(b=>b.kind==='ink'&&b.ws==='r10'). Ink records are {kind:'ink', pts:[[x,y,pressure,nibFactor]], w,h,width,color,style,x,y,z:500,group}. db.js exports childBlocks/levelEdges/allByWs over those indexes. (G:/Other computers/My Computer/LAT_WorkSpace/Notes/Interface/js/db.js (whole file); scratchpad r10test.ps1, r6test.ps1, audit.ps1, fix3test.ps1) => 2.0 must keep the IndexedDB name, store names and the block record shape (including the 4-tuple pts and the nib pad convention) or every one of the 25 scripts needs its seed rewritten; this is also the file-format contract (workspacePayload version 2).
- [high] 15 of the 25 active scripts depend on committed ink being a '.block-ink' DOM element (70 literal references), across six coupling classes: element counts, getBoundingClientRect, dispatching pointer events on the element, reading the SVG path/attrs or rasterising the SVG, selection classes/computed filter on the element, and #ink-live 2D probes. - Per-script '.block-ink' counts: r10 5, shape2 1, r6 1, r5 2, r4 6, r3 2, r2 7, r2b 4, audit 1, drawtest 9, pentest 10, tooltest 8, verifytest 8, matchtest 1, perfbench 5; fix3test reaches the ink element via '.block[data-id=k1]'. Ten scripts have no ink-DOM coupling (r11, r9, r7, shapebench, r1, r1p, b1, b23, sheettest, pdftest). Dispatch-on-ink-element sites: r3 x3, r5 x2, r6 x2, tooltest x4, pentest, r10, drawtest, verifytest, fix3. SVG rasterisation for pixel comparison: r6test finalMask, matchtest finalThicknessAt. (grep over scratchpad r*test.ps1, drawtest.ps1, pentest.ps1, tooltest.ps1, verifytest.ps1, matchtest.ps1, audit.ps1, perfbench.ps1 (see table in summary)) => This is the exact blast radius of moving committed ink to a canvas; each class maps to one __ng call (inks(), inkScreenRect(), hitTest()/stage dispatch, strokePathD()/renderStroke()/readInkPixels(), selection()/selectionMode(), readLivePixels()).
- [high] In 11 of the 15 coupled scripts a missing '.block-ink' element aborts the whole script (no try/catch, so a null .getBoundingClientRect()/.dataset throws and Runtime.evaluate returns EXC); those 11 scripts hold 467 of ~830 keys (56%). Only shape2, drawtest, pentest, tooltest and fix3 degrade to PARTIAL. - Scripts without 'catch(err)': r10test, r6test, r5test, r4test, r3test, r2test, r2btest, audit, verifytest, matchtest, perfbench (key counts 44+27+64+101+36+33+23+21+10+2+106). perfbench's B.openWs additionally loops up to 60 s waiting for document.querySelectorAll('#world .block').length to reach the expected count, which can never be met if ink is not a .block element. (scratchpad r10test.ps1 (no try), r7test.ps1 line 'catch(err)' pattern; perfbench.ps1 B.openWs) => A naive 2.0 drop-in would not fail 150 assertions, it would silently lose more than half the suite's output; the port must land the __ng shim before the renderer change, and ported bodies should adopt the PARTIAL try/catch pattern.
- [high] Committed strokes today are one <div class='block block-ink' data-id style='left/top/z-index:500'><svg class='ink-svg'><path d=...></svg></div> each (3 DOM nodes per stroke), rebuilt by paintInkNode with the same inkStrokeD generator the live canvas uses; selection adds a two-pass drop-shadow filter per stroke, replaced by a dashed outline via #world.many-sel above 12 selected. - makeBlockEl app.js:538-565 (INK_Z=500 at 5715); paintInkNode 754-773 (svg reuse in place when possible; applyInkStyle 247-262 sets fill/stroke/opacity/dasharray/mixBlendMode); CSS .block.block-ink.selected filter: drop-shadow x2 at styles.css:464-465; #world.many-sel override 468-469; applySelectionClasses toggles 'selected' on every element in state.els and sets many-sel at sel.size>12 (app.js:1995-2007). perfbench P3 confirms nodes_added_by_300_strokes=900. (G:/Other computers/My Computer/LAT_WorkSpace/Notes/Interface/js/app.js:538-565, 754-773, 247-262, 1995-2007; css/styles.css:461-469) => Defines what the suite currently observes (svg node identity in r10 inkSvgReused, path d in verifytest/drawtest, computed filter in tooltest/r10) and what a canvas renderer must reproduce through __ng.strokePathD/strokeStyle/selectionMode instead of DOM.
- [high] Live-preview cost scales with stroke length and dpr squared because paintLiveStroke clears the whole full-stage canvas and re-strokes the entire in-progress stroke as one Path2D on every paint (up to 2 paints per frame). - paintLiveStroke app.js:4171-4192: clearInkSurface(); new Path2D(inkStrokeD(pts,...)); ctx.stroke/fill of the whole path. scheduleLivePaint 4198-4207 paints synchronously on the first sample of a frame and once more on the next rAF. sizeInkSurface caps dpr at 2.5 (4141). perfbench P1 raster-flush probes (getImageData 1x1 after a sample) grow 1.3 -> 2.0 -> 2.6 ms at samples ~150/750/1450 at dpr 1 and 3.3 -> 4.9 -> 7.5 ms at dpr 2.5 on a desktop headless Edge; per-move dispatch p95 grows 0.2 -> 0.5 -> 0.9 ms across the same segments (move_100_200 vs move_1400_1500). (app.js:4171-4207, 4137-4145; scratchpad perfbench-final.txt lines P1 and P1b_dpr2.5) => Directly behind complaint (1) on a slower tablet GPU at dpr 2.5 (3600x2090 buffer): a long word costs 7+ ms of raster per paint on the desktop, so 2.0 should paint only the new segment(s) into a persistent live layer (or tile) and keep the whole-stroke repaint for pan/zoom only; the new test #2 must measure time-to-first-pixel within one frame.
- [medium] The pointer-intake de-duplication drops any coalesced pointermove sample whose timeStamp is <= the last accepted sample once any pointerrawupdate has been seen for the stroke; genuinely new samples with equal or earlier timestamps are lost, and the existing r10test cannot detect this because its synthetic coalesced list reuses the very same event objects as the raw channel. - addInkSamples app.js:4228-4257: 'if (!fromRaw && inking.rawSeen && t <= inking.lastT) continue;' plus a 0.7px/scale minStep filter (4233, 4240). r10test section A dispatches 60 pointerrawupdate events and then one pointermove whose coalescedEvents are those same 60 objects (identical timeStamp and coordinates), so dropping them is correct there and the test passes with 55..62 pts. On Chromium/Android the raw channel can itself be coalesced when the main thread is busy while the next pointermove's coalesced list still carries the intermediate digitiser samples with timestamps <= lastT (and ms-quantised equal timestamps), which this rule discards - consistent with 'points skipped and the stroke connects straight to where the pen ended'. (app.js:4222-4257 (comment at 4222-4226 states the design intent); scratchpad r10test.ps1 section A) => Inferred root of complaint (2). 2.0 should de-duplicate by sample identity (x,y,t,pressure) or by a per-stroke monotone sequence merged from both channels, never by 't <= lastT'; new intake tests (i)-(iv) and on-device golden traces via __ng.recordSamples are required because synthetic events cannot reproduce the Android delivery pattern.
- [high] Group drag of many selected strokes writes style.left/top on every selected element per pointermove and then immediately measures getBoundingClientRect() on every selected element to reposition #sel-frame and #sel-bar, forcing a full layout per move; lasso selection reads offsetWidth/offsetHeight for every block on the level and toggles a class on every element. - onPointerMove dragging branch app.js:4668-4695: loop sets el.style.left/top for each id, then positionSelFrame() -> selectionWorldBox() (1833-1850: getBoundingClientRect per selected element) and positionSelBar() (1800-1815: getBoundingClientRect per selected element again), then drawEdges() or scheduleMinimap(). selectInsideLasso 3681-3700 uses el.offsetWidth/offsetHeight per block; applySelectionClasses 1998 toggles 'selected' across all state.els. perfbench P6 (906 blocks, 91..132 selected per lasso): lasso_up_select 10.3 ms median / 20.9 max and LayoutCount +2005, RecalcStyleCount +2424 for 50 lassos (~40 layouts per lasso). (app.js:4668-4695, 1800-1855, 3681-3700, 1995-2007; perfbench-final.txt P6 and METRICS[after_P6]) => Mechanism behind complaint (3): cost is O(selected) writes + O(selected) forced reads per move plus O(level) style recalc, all on a slow tablet with blurred per-stroke filters below the 12-stroke threshold. 2.0 should keep selection geometry in data (no DOM measurement) and move ink via layer/tile offsets; new test #4 asserts LayoutCount delta == 0 during a 300-stroke drag.
- [high] Per-frame cost already grows with page size in the current architecture: zoom-step forced style+layout flush 13.2 ms median at 606 blocks -> 35.9 ms at 2206 blocks; minimap redraw 2.1 -> 7.1 ms per frame; undo p95 32 ms -> 74 ms; workspace open 174 -> 577 ms. - perfbench P2 vs P8: zoom_in_forced_style_layout_flush_ms med13.2/max24.4 vs med35.9/max49.7; minimap_draw_ms med2.1/max3.4 vs med7.1/max11.7 (drawMinimap app.js:5847 iterates every block and calls blockRectOf on each pan/zoom frame); undo_ms med16.5/p95 32.4 at 906 blocks vs med9.3/p95 74.4 at 2206; open_ms 174 vs 577. applyView (442-460) sets --inv on #world per zoom step, restyling every block's handles (comment at 446-449). (perfbench-final.txt P2, P8, METRICS lines; app.js:442-460, 5847-5890) => Quantifies the 'per-frame cost independent of how much is on the page' target: 2.0 acceptance should be <1.3x growth from 606 to 10 000 strokes for pan/zoom/minimap/undo (new test #7), which a DOM-per-stroke design cannot meet.
- [high] Autosave serialises the entire workspace to one JSON file on every debounced change: 2207 blocks with 39 430 ink points and 1.38 MB of image data URLs produce a 2.42 MB payload (IDB readAll 74 ms + stringify 7-13 ms on desktop) written through File System Access or NGShell.writeFile. - markChanged app.js:7050-7060 schedules autosaveTick after 900 ms (due within 10 s, deferred while a gesture is live: 6946-6952); saveWorkspaceNow 7005-7048 -> workspacePayload 6147-6172 reads allByWs blocks/edges/files and JSON.stringify's everything. perfbench P7: json_bytes=2449345, payload_bytes=2420826, idb_readAll_ms=74.1, payload_build_stringify_ms=13.1. (app.js:6147-6172, 6946-6952, 7005-7060; perfbench-final.txt P7) => On the tablet this write lands a few seconds after each stroke and competes with inking; 2.0 should keep the file format but make the serialisation incremental/off-thread (new test #8), and the suite's savetest/importtest already pin the file-format behaviours to preserve.
- [high] Six scripts read #ink-live pixels through a 2D context (r9, r6, matchtest, verifytest, perfbench, plus non-active livetest/perftest) and r10 counts live paints by monkey-patching CanvasRenderingContext2D.prototype.stroke/fill filtered on canvas.id==='ink-live'; perftest also asserts getContextAttributes().desynchronized. - r9 litPixels alpha>25 count >200 at move 50; r6 liveMask alpha>40 over a stage region at dpr; matchtest column-run thickness; verifytest sampled alpha; perfbench getImageData(0,0,1,1) as a raster-flush timer; r10 paintsSync<=1 && paintsAfterFrame<=2. The canvas is display:none unless #stage.penning (styles.css:329-335) and sized to min(devicePixelRatio,2.5). (scratchpad r9test.ps1, r6test.ps1, matchtest.ps1, verifytest.ps1, r10test.ps1 section B, perfbench.ps1 B.stroke flushAt; app.js:4129-4145; css/styles.css:329-335) => If 2.0 moves the live layer to WebGL, OffscreenCanvas or a worker these probes break; keep the element id #ink-live and expose __ng.readLivePixels()/live().mode/counters().livePaints so the same assertions can be re-expressed.
- [high] The eraser already hit-tests ink from data rather than DOM for sweep and lasso modes (point-to-segment distance on world-space points with densification), while the whole-stroke eraser and tap selection still rely on elementFromPoint/.closest('.block-ink'). - eraseSweep app.js:5445-5475 uses inkWorldPts + distToSeg with bbox pre-filter and splits runs into new blocks; eraseInsideLasso 5481-5493 samples 24 points per stroke through pointInPoly; eraseStrokeAt 5409-5416 uses document.elementFromPoint(...).closest('.block-ink'). r4test normalEraserSplits/undoRejoins and r10test eraseThenUndoRestores pin the split/undo semantics via IDB pts counts. (app.js:5409-5493; scratchpad r4test.ps1 (erasers section), r10test.ps1 section F) => Reusable as the basis of __ng.hitTest and 2.0's stage-level stroke picking; the split/undo behaviours are already tested through IDB, so they port with no change.
- [high] Shape recognition is already exposed as a test API (window.__ngShape = {recognizeShape, srMatch, wiggle, tidyClosed, resamplePts}) and shapebench drives it with 41 deterministic point sets without touching the DOM. - app.js:4074 assigns the hook; shapebench.ps1 builds jittered circles/polygons/lines/scribbles with a seeded LCG and logs accuracy N/41 plus avg ms per stroke; the last run reported no fails. (app.js:4074; scratchpad shapebench.ps1; suite-final.txt 'shapebench:' line) => Template for the proposed window.__ng: pure-function hooks are the only part of the suite that is renderer-independent today, and 2.0 should keep __ngShape unchanged.
- [high] The functional suite spends about 198 s in literal wait() sleeps (r4 25.9 s, r5 16.7 s, r10 14.9 s) plus roughly 6 s per script for Edge boot and navigation, so a full run is about 6 minutes, and many sleeps exist only to let finalizeInk's un-awaited IDB write and history record land. - Sum of wait(n) literals across the 24 functional scripts = 197 660 ms; each script sleeps 3 s after Page.navigate and polls up to 12 s for the CDP page. finalizeInk (app.js:5717-5762) is called without await from onPointerUp (4801) and records history inside afterInkWrites (1503-1508); tests therefore wait 300-900 ms before reading IDB or pressing undo. (grep of wait( across scratchpad active scripts; app.js:1503-1508, 4784-4803, 5717-5762) => A __ng.flush() that resolves after the ink write chain plus one frame would remove most sleeps, cut the suite to roughly a minute, and make timing-sensitive assertions (undo right after lift, live-cleared-after-commit) deterministic.
- [low] Rendering artefacts during large lasso selections are plausibly caused by hundreds of simultaneous filter/outline changes on SVG elements inside a will-change:transform #world layer, but this could not be verified from the code or the headless benchmarks. - styles.css:464-469 applies drop-shadow filters (below 12 selected) or a dashed outline on every selected .ink-svg (above 12); #world has will-change: transform (styles.css:592); applySelectionClasses toggles every element's class in one pass. perfbench P6 shows RecalcStyleDuration +1089 ms over 50 lassos at 906 blocks on desktop but cannot observe GPU tile or compositor glitches. (css/styles.css:461-469, 592; app.js:1995-2007; perfbench-final.txt METRICS[after_P6]) => Supports rendering selection highlight into the ink layer itself (data-driven, tile-local) rather than per-element CSS; new test #5 checks that highlight changes pixels only inside selected bboxes.

Recommendations:
- Ship window.__ng (state/inks/selection/inkScreenRect/hitTest/strokePathD/strokeStyle/renderStroke/readLivePixels/readInkPixels/tiles/counters/flush/injectSamples) in the 1.x codebase first, next to window.__ngShape at app.js:4074, and port the 15 coupled scripts to it while the DOM renderer is still in place; only then swap the renderer. This turns the renderer change into a green-to-green diff instead of 56% of keys going dark.
- Keep unchanged: IndexedDB name/stores/record shape (4-tuple pts, nib pad = width+2, z 500), the workspace JSON format (app:'NotesGallery', version 2), every non-ink DOM surface the suite touches (#brand/.ws-card, toolbar and panel ids, #stage classes penning/erasing/erase-normal/lassoing/panning, #world transform + --inv + .many-sel class, #sel-frame/.sel-grip/#sel-bar, #toast, #fatal-banner, #import-input/#image-input), the #ink-live element id with a readable 2D fallback, and window.__ngShape. 10 of 25 active scripts then need no change at all.
- Replace the timestamp de-dup in addInkSamples with identity-based merging of the raw and coalesced channels (key on x,y,timeStamp,pressure, or keep a per-pointer ring of the last N raw samples and drop only exact matches), and never discard samples on '<='. Record real S-Pen traces on the Samsung through __ng.recordSamples() and add them as golden replays; synthetic PointerEvents cannot reproduce Android batching.
- Paint the live stroke incrementally into a persistent live layer (segment appends, whole-stroke repaint only on pan/zoom/resize) and cap the live buffer at the visible stage; assert time-to-first-pixel within one rAF for every style/blend/dpr, since perfbench shows whole-stroke repaint cost growing linearly with points and quadratically with dpr.
- Move selection geometry, hit-testing and drag to data: compute the selection box from block records (not getBoundingClientRect), move ink by adjusting layer/tile offsets, and repaint only touched tiles; gate this with the new large-selection test (LayoutCount delta == 0 during a 300-stroke drag, per-move median <= 2 ms).
- Make the committed layer tiled with versioned tiles and expose __ng.tiles(); add invalidation tests (commit/erase/undo/move/zoom touch only intersecting tiles, eviction cap) and a scale test at 606/2206/10 000 strokes requiring < 1.3x growth in pan/zoom/minimap/undo cost.
- Port r6test and matchtest to __ng.readInkPixels() instead of rasterising a cloned SVG; this tests the actual committed pixels (stronger than today) and removes the last dependency on SVG serialisation.
- Add __ng.flush() and replace the ~400 wait(n) calls; keep the PARTIAL try/catch pattern (r7/drawtest style) in every ported body so a single null read reports partial results instead of EXC.
- Make autosave incremental or off-thread but byte-compatible: after one stroke the write should be proportional to the stroke, and importtest/savetest must still pass unchanged.
- Extend the CDP harness with Performance.getMetrics deltas (LayoutCount, RecalcStyleCount) around gestures as pass/fail thresholds, not just logs, so layout thrash regressions fail the suite.
Open questions:
- Does the Android WebView used by Tauri 2 on the Samsung tablet deliver pointerrawupdate at digitiser rate, only per frame, or not at all, and do its coalesced samples carry ms-quantised (equal) timestamps? A recorded trace via __ng.recordSamples() would settle whether the 't <= lastT' rule is the cause of complaint (2).
- Does getContext('2d', {desynchronized:true}) actually engage on Android WebView (SurfaceView/TextureView compositing), and is the 'preview sometimes missing entirely' tied to the highlighter's mix-blend-mode on the canvas or to the desynchronized swap path? perftest's desynchronized flag has only been read in headless Edge.
- Will 2.0 keep the ink-selection frame and lasso path as DOM/SVG (#sel-frame, .lasso-path) or move them into the canvas layer? The suite reads #sel-frame rects and .lasso-path presence in 9 scripts; keeping them as DOM is cheap and preserves those keys.
- Should 2.0 keep lightweight per-stroke ghost elements (positioned divs without SVG) for hit-testing and selection classes, which would keep coupling classes 1-3 and 5 working without a port but reintroduce O(n) style/layout on --inv and class toggles, or go fully data-driven behind __ng?
- What are the acceptance thresholds on the actual tablet (frame gaps, time-to-first-pixel, drag handler ms)? All current numbers come from headless desktop Edge at dpr 1/2.5 and are lower bounds; the perfbench harness cannot run on the device, so an on-device __ng.counters() dump is needed to calibrate.
- Does the PNG/SVG/PDF export path (b23test, pdftest) need ink rendered from data on the export canvas, and should a pixel assertion for exported ink be added since none exists today?
